# A Small Favor - Louis Stull

**Level:** 50 | **Maximum Level:** 130 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 50
**Maximum Level:** 130
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo

Poison Master Feilicks may be found at /waypoint +551, +214, -34 in North Kaladim. He is on the find tool.
Poison Master Tenk may be found at /waypoint +46, +188, -29 in North Qeynos.
Poison Master Skuk may be found at /waypoint -480, -1155, -137 inside the Masters of the Reach house in the Dragon's Grove in Crescent Reach.
You say, 'Hail, Poison Master Skuk'
Poison Master Skuk looks you up and down as if assessing your worth. 'Hello there, _____. It's good that you have come to me today. I was just saying to myself, Self, I said, who is worthy of this [opportunity] that I could offer? And now, here you are!'
You say, 'What opportunity?'
Poison Master Skuk nods. 'Yes, a most fantastic opportunity. I happen to have in my possession certain compounds. These compounds, when combined together, form [toxins] of various types. You might apply one of these toxics to a blade and if your hand happened to slip and the blade pierced the side of your enemy, why that enemy might begin to suffer in terrible agony. And that would be a terrible shame, wouldn't it?'
You say, 'What toxins?'
Poison Master Skuk laughs. 'You're interested? Of course you are! But I'm a businessman first and foremost. You would need to perform a [service] for me first. Consider it a small token of your appreciation for the gift that I will bestow upon you.'
You say, 'What service?'
Poison Master Skuk smiles grimly, the glint in his eye hinting at what a dangerous person you are dealing with. 'Here is your task, young one. Do not disappoint me. Never forget the tools of your craft - you must be invisible to detection or you will surely die. And you're no good to me dead!'
You have been assigned the task 'A Small Favor - Louis Stull'.
The Poison Master has asked you to do a simple task and promised ample rewards for your small service.  Travel to West Freeport and resolve a small misunderstanding between the Poison Master and one Louis Stull.
It would be in your best interests to be hidden from view while undertaking this task as the Poison Master's informants report the Freeport Militia are on high alert.

# Underhanded Exploration

**Level:** 40 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** No | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 40
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** No
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo

You say, 'Hail, Cadale Brohat'
Cadale Brohat smiles widely at you. 'Welcome. You are brave to venture to Broken Skull Rock. Don't mind the others. Everyone is a bit touchy around here. If you are interested in dyes, feel free to peruse what I have to offer. Playing with them has given me hours of entertainment! Just be careful on this rock, and watch your back. Oh, before I forget, I'm looking for someone to help me with some [tasks] that I need to get done. All my time with the dyes has kelp me from some of the more important things I need to take care of..'
You say, 'Tasks?'
You have been assigned the task 'Underhanded Exploration'.
Have you ever felt the power rushing through your body as you slam down a sword on a slavering mongel? Perhaps you've wanted to feel the surge of excitement as a burst of magic explodes from your fingertips and eviscerates a worthless bag of bones? Now is your chance. You've been entered in the Go Anywhere, Kill Anything contest being held by the Wayfarer's Brotherhood! To guarantee your entry into the event, your going to need to kill 10 subverted miners, and loot 4 Ornate Miner's Pick. A Wayfarer's Brotherhood representative will use these as your proof of entry. Good Luck!
Kill 10 subverted miners - 0/10 - Torgiran Mines
Loot 4 Ornate Miner's Pick - 0/4 - Torgiran Mines
Your task 'Underhanded Exploration' has been updated
The next stage of the contest is to explore the pier in the southwest section of the crypt. This is your chance to fulfill the main portion of the 'Go Anywhere' part of the contest. Have fun with this one.
Explore the pier in the southwest section of the crypt - 0/1 - Crypt of Nadox
The task updates when you get close to  -1126, 1449, -129.
You're almost halfway done with the contest and your odds of winning are quite good. All you need to do now to remain an active participant is to kill 10 shady treasure sorters. Get out there and Kill Anything!
Kill 10 shady treasure sorters - 0/10 - Dulak's Harbor
Your task 'Underhanded Exploration' has been updated.
The second part of the 'Go Anywhere' portion of the event will require you to deliver 4 Ornate Miner's Pick to Fellgandran. Keep up the pace, you're past the halfway point.
Explore the western courtyard door in the temple - 0/1 - Gulf of Gunthak
This updates near -2807, 3035, 479.
You're in the homestretch now, just one last thing to do! You need to get moving and speak with Calambra. They're waiting for you!
Speak with Calambra - 0/1 - Gulf of Gunthak
You can find Calambra at /waypoint 1492, -1053, 44 on the top deck of the ship in The Gulf of Gunthak.
You say, 'Hail, Calambra'
Your task 'Underhanded Exploration' has been updated.
Well, unfortunately, you didn't come in first, but you were close! You did an excellent job and nearly beat all the competition. Don't give up. You'll do much better next time! You did do quite well though, so here's your prize money. Better luck next time!
You receive 3 copper
You receive 7 silver
You receive 1 gold
You receive 176 platinum
You gain experience!!

# The Realm of Pain

**Level:** 50 | **Maximum Level:** 130 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** No | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 50
**Maximum Level:** 130
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** No
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Quest
**Group Size:** Solo

This is one of the finished Kunark quests added on the February 15, 2017, update.
Find Zreksan in the East Cabilis monk guild, /waypoint +253, -47
You say, 'Hail, Zreksan'
Zreksan gives a short nod of his head and wide swoop of his tail in greetings to Bobbybick, 'Yes? You must have come in search of something, have you not? Perhaps you seek to further your knowledge of [the realm of Pain:I do seek to further my knowledge of the realm of Pain.], yes?'
You say, 'I do seek to further my knowledge of the realm of Pain.'
Zreksan says, 'Of course you have, Bobbybick, and I shall be more than willing to oblige your search. First, you must trek deep into the Burning Woods and find one known as Nadira. She is a human monk, one who has come to our homeland in the hopes of vanquishing our people because she feels our art is only worthy to those who follow the way of tranquility. Reave her of that thought, and all thoughts, Bobbybick. Return to me her femur as proof of your success in this task. I shall also need you to acquire Goblin Bone Beads from the goblin mines and a Fine Sheet of Black Ore from the goblin temple.'
Fine Sheet of Black Ore drops from named goblins in revamped Droga or Soothsayer Dregzak in Droga 1.0, Goblin Bone Beads drop from "The Bone Seer" in the Mines of Nurga, and Nadira's Femur comes from Nadira who wanders the eastern side of Burning Woods.
Hand Zreksan the Sheet of Black Ore, Goblin Bone Beads, and Nadira's Femur.
Zreksan says, 'Well done, Bobbybick. I do hope that Nadira's demise was a most educational one for you in teaching the ways of pain.' The monk chuckles, a hissing, sinister laughter, as he eyes the femur cradled within his clutching claws, 'I have secured our... departed Soldier of Tranquility's remains into a staff that shall be the beginning of your furthered knowledge in our ways, Bobbybick. It is incomplete, however, and we must enlist the assistance of one known as Tikesh. She shall aid in furthering its completion. Take the staff, as well as a Small Froglok Bone Vial and the Blood of the Arisen Soldier, to Tikesh.'
Receive: Decorative Bone Staff
Tikesh can be found inside the Court of Pain, the Cabilis Monk Guild.
You say, 'Hail, Tikesh'
No response to hails.
Small Froglok Bone Vial can be found from Krup Ghoul Knights in the crypt of Sebilis (requires a lockpicker), and the Blood of the Arisen Soldier comes from "The Arisen Soldier" a named skeleton that spawns just inside the gates of Karnor's Castle.
Give the Decorative Bone Staff, Small Froglok Bone Vial, and Blood of the Arisen Soldier to Tikesh.
Tikesh says, 'I see that Zreksan has removed the pest in the Burning Wood, yes? The news that Nadira has been taught the ways of pain is quite pleasing. Aaah, and you have brought me bone and blood! How marvelous! Allow me to apply a series of blood runes to your staff... and my work is complete, Bobbybick. Should you wish to improve the staff further, return it to Zreksan along with the Chant of Pain, an item lost to our Order for some time.'
Receive: Blood Runed Staff
Chant of Pain can be found on "Pain-Wracked Skeleton" which spawns in Howling stones (confirmed East Wing unknown if it can appear elsewhere).
Turn in Blood Runed Staff and the Chant of Pain to Zreksan in the Cabilis monk guild.
Zreksan says, 'Well done, Bobbybick. Well done indeed.' Zreksan holds the staff in one hand and the chant in the other. As he reads the chant, the beads upon the staff begin to sway, as if an ethereal wind were passing through the chamber. As the chant fades to an end, all settles and is calm. He hands you the staff and grins, 'Your instruction in pain continues, Bobbybick. Consider this staff your teacher and heed its lessons.'
Receive: Staff of Pain
Your faction standing with Swift Tails has been adjusted by 5.
Your faction standing with Legion of Cabilis has been adjusted by 1.
- Staff of Pain
- Kunark Archaeologist player title suffix if first per server
Submitted by: BobbybickRewards:
- Staff of Pain [item=128063]
[

# Subversion

**Level:** 50 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Time:** 60
**Level:** 50
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Quest
**Group Size:** Solo

This task was available at level 50
Assistant Vagli can be found in the pirate village in Gulf of Gunthak at location 1385, 75, 3.
Entry for map file:
P -75.1076, -1387.0557, 0.0020, 240, 240, 0, 3, Assistant_Vagli
You say, 'Hail, Assistant Vagli'
Assistant Vagli says 'Greetings, friend. If you have come to study under the great Alvarik you have come to the right place! I help by carrying his research tomes for him. If there is one you would like to buy, feel free to browse through what we have. If you're looking for something to do, I may have a [task] available for you to get started on. Alvarik has much he needs done and I can't do it all myself!'
You say, 'What task?'
He offers you 3 tasks, one of which is 'Subversion:
Medium length task, begins in Dulak's Harbor
Objective Instructions
- Explore the ship Oceancrasher at one of the eastern docks.
- Kill 10 subverted miners (Torgiran Mines)
- Loot 4 Pristine Ore Sample (Torgiran Mines)
- Explore Overlod Ngrub's room in the east halls of the mine complex (loc for update was +1255, -1306, -146)
- Deliver 4 Pristine Ore Sample to Calambra (Gulf of Gunthak) (on the boat beside the light house at loc +1480, -1043, +41)
Task description in Quest Journal:
There are books that speak of a place of mystical origins. It has yet to be found, but the belief is that you can find it if you explore the ship Oceancrasher at one of the eastern docks. Stay on your guard while you search for these mystical ruins, for there are stones of someone... or something that doesn't want you around.
* Pristine Ore Sample is a No Drop item that can be obtained from an mob with "miner" in its name.
Upon turn in,
Your task 'Subversion' has been updated.
Your task 'Subversion' has been updated.
Your task 'Subversion' has been updated.
Your task 'Subversion' has been updated.
This is a historic day for literary minds all over the world. You were finally able to uncover the truth behind the mystical ruins and the beasts that guard it. You must have been terribly fascinated and frightened by the whole prospect, but you certainly kepy your wits about you. The literary society is willing to offer you a good fee for collecting those relics, so please do accept this.
You receive Experience
You receive 7 copper
You receive 2 silver
You receive 4 gold
You receive 54 platinum Submitted by: SentientShamanRewards:
[

# Dulak Bandits

**Level:** 1 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** No | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 1
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** No
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Quest
**Group Size:** Solo

You say, 'Hail, Assistant Vagli'
Assistant Vagli says 'Greetings, friend. If you have come to study under the great Alvarik you have come to the right place! I help by carrying his research tomes for him. If there is one you would like to buy, feel free to browse through what we have. If you're looking for something to do, I may have a [task] available for you to get started on. Alvarik has much he needs done and I can't do it all myself!'
You say, 'What task?'
He offers you 3 tasks, one of which is 'Dulak Bandits'
Medium length task, begins in Gulf of Gunthak
Objective Instructions
- Speak with Welligorn (Gunthank beach at -205, -672, -34)
- Explore the ship Stormwave at one of the southern docks (Dulak's Harbor)
- Kill 10 Broken Skull brigands (Dulak's Harbor - found within the cabins along the south side of the docks)
- Speak with Zalil Nyvin (Plane of Knowledge - Inside of a building on the north side of PoK south of the Kelethin stone)
Task description in Quest Journal:
It's difficult to keep a farm running on a daily basis, what with all the trouble farmers face today. One thing they can count on is vicious predators on the prowl for their next meal, usually livestock. There's one farmer that's tired of it and wants help. Go speak with Welligorn, for more information.
Upon hailing Zalil Hyvin;
Thanks for contacting me, ______. Your information on this matter has been most useful.
You receive Experience
You receive 9 copper
You receive 1 silver
You receive 7 gold
You receive 45 platinumSubmitted by: SentientShamanRewards:
- Experience, Coin.

**Level:** 1
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** No

**Quest Type:** Achievement
**Group Size:** Solo

This achievement is gained upon defeating the following rare monster in The Gulf of Gunthak:
Blood Tusk
a dastardly scoundrel
Dominator Zrabix
Dreadmaster Jrup
Guard Dragrik
Harbinger Stimika
Hatethorn
a human recruit
Kalii Brokenskull
Magus Evana Bavomon
Mokon Brokenskull
a mutant cohort
an old sea dog
Oracle Pagrossa
Preceptor Grakomus
Rite Master Kvimimn
Sea Captain Azobus
Soothsayer Mrugg Doxok
Treasure Hunter Eranil
Warmaster Mragoc
a dastardly scoundrel loc -2273, -237
In a small hut S of the fort enterance, closest to the S wall.
a human recruit loc -2894, 2836
In castle, first big room through west door.
a mutant cohort loc -2905, 2689
In castle, big room from main doors. SW corner. Closest to stairs.
an old sea dog loc -1668, -414
3 mob spawn outside of fort. Near the NE corner. Of the 3, spawn was furthest from the fort wall.
Blood Tusk loc -876, 1981
In the village, big house with 4 boars. PH is always on the bed.
Dominator Zrabix loc -350, 2829
NW corner of the village. Of the 3 luggald spawn it was the west mob.
Dreadmaster Jrup loc -2911, 2302
In castle, big room to the right of first room from main doors. SE corner.
Guard Dragrik loc -2222, 1529
In tunnel on left just before Crypt of Nadox zone out.
Harbinger Stimika loc -2994, 2806
In castle. Enter through west door. 2nd big room, NE corner.
Hatethorn loc -745, 1165
Second to last plant on left side of tunnel before you enter the village area.
Kalii Brokenskull loc -1877, -234
Inside round building in fort at throne.
Magus Evana Bavomon loc -1052, 1724
In a big house in the village near the SE corner.
Mokon Brokenskull loc -188, -1030
E side of the beach near the water.
Oracle Pagrossa loc -778, 1784
As you enter the village from the tunnel, first big house on your left. Troll just inside the door on the right.
Preceptor Grakomus loc -3905, 2537
In the castle, in throne room, on the throne.
Rite Master Kvimimn loc -1913, -259
In round building with Kalii Brokenskull spawn. a Luggald PH.
Sea Captain Azobus loc -1217, -933
Inside the broken ship in the SE corner of beach area.
Soothsayer Hunter Doxok roamer in fort.
Treasure Hunter Eranil loc -433, -825
His PH's are a cast out survivor, a Broken Skull scorpion, a Broken Skull mosquito, a Broken Skull nifilik.
You will find by killing these they respawn as different ph. My loc for TH Eranil was one possible.
Did not get it to respawn to confirm that it was only spawn site. Also spawns by fort too.
Warmaster Mragoc loc -551, 2309
From the far NW corner of village 4th hut.

# Nurga's Finest

**Level:** 1 | **Maximum Level:** 130 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** No | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 1
**Maximum Level:** 130
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** No
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Quest
**Group Size:** Solo

This is a new quest added in the May 2018 Patch.
You will need AT LEAST apprehensive faction for this quest (Tested and confirmed that Dubious will not work and Indifferent will work).
Zusuu the Mineralist is a common spawn in the western section of the Mines of Nurga, he has many potential placeholders.
You say, 'Hail, Zusuu the Mineralist'
Zusuu the Mineralist says, 'Hello there, Bobbybick.  I trust you are here to assist with the mining.  I am just now in the process of [cleansing] a new batch of stones.'
You say, 'Cleansing?'
Zusuu the Mineralist says, 'Well... it is actually quite a funny story. Barkeep Sagaag created a special drink.  It doesn't go down smoothly, but the aftertaste has a great kick to it.  Oddly enough when I accidentally spilt my drink on a bag of stones one day, they began to glimmer.  Ever since then, I dip the rare stones of the Mines of Nurga in Sagaag's Finest to give them a glowing coat.  I seem to be out of Sagaag's Finest though. Perhaps if someone can ask him for some, I can continue this process.'
Sagaag can be found in the eastern section of Nurga, just past the dropdown into the pool of water from Frontier Mountains.
You say, 'Hail, Sagaag'
Sagaag says, 'Hi there. Sagaag mix good drinks. You drink some and you will see. Try the Nurgan Herbal Ale. It tastes real good.  At least that's what they tell me.'
You say, 'finest'
Sagaag says, 'Sagaag can't make Sagaag's Finest right now.  Sagaag needs some of Poguu's Herbal Growth to make it. Bring Sagaag the herbs and Sagaag make good drink for you.'
Poguu the Herbalist is a common spawn in the Western/Central section of Nurga.  Like Zusuu, he has many potential placeholders.
You say, 'Hail, Poguu the Herbalist'
Poguu the Herbalist says, 'Greetings, Bobbybick. Speak to my assistant if you wish to purchase some herbs or potions.'
You say, 'Herbal Growth'
Poguu the Herbalist says, 'In order to get the herbal growth to rise, I will need some Mt. Death Mineral Salts to spread over the crops.  Bring me four piles of the salt from the Drogans and I will be able to cultivate my herbs.'
Mt. Death Mineral Salts are a common drop from any goblin in Droga and revamped nurga.
Give 4 Mt. Death Mineral Salts to Poguu the Herbalist.
Poguu the Herbalist says, 'Ah, you found the salt. I will use it to plow the field... There, now my herbal growth will rise.'
Your faction standing with Goblins of Mountain Death has been adjusted by 10.
Your faction standing with Brood of Di`Zok has been adjusted by -1.
Receive: Poguu's Herbal Growth
Give Poguu's Herbal Growth to Sagaag
Sagaag says, 'Just what Sagaag needs to make Sagaa's Finest! Here you are.'
Your faction standing with Goblins of Mountain Death has been adjusted by 10.
Your faction standing with Brood of Di`Zok has been adjusted by -1.
Receive: Sagaag's Finest
Give Sagaag's Finest to Zusuu the Mineralist
Zusuu the Mineralist says, 'Excellent! Thank you for bringing me a fresh supply of Sagaag's Finest. Now I will attempt to coat the stones. It doesn't always work the same, but I will give you the outcome of the dipping.'
Your faction standing with Goblins of Mountain Death has been adjusted by 50.
Your faction standing with Brood of Di`Zok has been adjusted by -2.
You gain experience!
Receive random mid-level gem (Star Ruby, Fire Opal, Peridot, Fire Emerald confirmed possible rewards)
Submitted by: BobbybickRewards:
- Fire Emerald [item=2833]
- Fire Opal [item=2836]
- Peridot [item=7783]
- Star Ruby [item=3142]
[

# Oh Captain...

**Level:** 1 | **Maximum Level:** 65 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 1
**Maximum Level:** 65
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Quest
**Group Size:** Solo

This task can be obtained from Cadale Brohat in Gulf of Gunthak at +1515, -244, +5 inside the light house in the far northern part of the zone. She is on the find tool.
There is a level limit on this task. I wasn't able to get it on a level 85 but was able to on a level 64.
You say, 'Hail, Cadale Brohat'
Cadale Brohat smiles widely at you. 'Welcome. You are brave to venture to Broken Skull Rock. Don't mind the others. Everyone is a bit touchy around here. If you are interested in dyes, feel free to peruse what I have to offer. Playing with them has given me hours of entertainment! Just be careful on this rock, and watch your back. Oh, before I forget, I'm looking for someone to help me with some [tasks] that I need to get done. All my time with the dyes has kept me from some of the more important things I need to take care of.'
You say, 'tasks'
You have been assigned the task 'Oh Captain...'.
1. Explore the captain's quarters 0/1 Hate's Fury
Task Window: You're about to embark on an adventure that will keep your sense clear and focused. There are no words to describe what you'll uncover, but know that when you arrive, you will find everything you had hoped for and more. To begin this amazing journey, just explore the captain's quarters. You'll have the time of your life.

# Stone Cold Summer: Skin So Soft [50]

**Level:** 26 | **Maximum Level:** 50 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 26
**Maximum Level:** 50
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This task is part of the Stone Cold Summer events that start in August.
You can obtain this task from Waagosh in The Feerrott (old zone) at /way 1765, -1754, 0 on the eastern side of the zone near the walking path. She is on the Find Tool (Ctrl+F).
NOTE: I got this task from both a level 40 and a level 26 characters. For now the Min/Max Level are set to 26 and 50, to be refined.
You say, 'Hail, Waagosh'
Waagosh says, 'Oh. Hello there. I didn't see you there. Are you [lost]?'
You say, 'No, I am not lost. I was just admiring your pet.'
Waagosh says, 'Ha. Ha. Ruugz? He's not my pet. I just need him to grow more, before I turn him into [a rug]. '
You say, 'A rug? That's just horrible.'
Waagosh says, 'He has [nice] scaly skin that feels good beneath my feet. The cold scaly skin helps sand off the extra bits between my toes.'
You say, 'That's nice, but did you need to name him?'
Waagosh says, 'Do you want to help [fatten him] up? '
You say, 'No, I do not want to help fatten him up.'
Waagosh says, 'Hrrm. Do you want to find some [replacement skins], to take his place?'
You say, 'Yes. I can find some replacement skins to take Ruugz's place.'
Waagosh says, 'Great. My feet need some help if I am going to finish my [investigations].'
You say, 'What investigations?'
You have been assigned the task 'Skin So Soft [50]'.
Waagosh says, 'Don't take too long. I would hate to have to finish my work with sore feet. '
Waagosh's feet have been bothering her, and she is looking for help to create a new scaly rug. She thinks it will take too long for Ruugz to grow to size, as she does have her investigation to get back to in the Feerrott.
Head to the Temple of Cazic Thule and hunt Tae Ew for strips of scaly skin.
Hunt jungle raptors that also call the temple home for their sinews.
In Waagosh's run sewing kit, combine five strips of scaly skin, two rough sinews, one simple sewing needle, and two aligned steel threads into one stitched scaly rug.
- 1. Loot a few strips of scaly skin 0/5 Temple of Cazic-Thule
These can drop off Tae ews.
- 2. Loot a few rough sinews 0/2 Temple of Cazic Thule
These can drop off jungle raptors.
- 3. Craft a new stitched scaly skin rug 0/1 ALL
In Waagosh's Rug Sewing kit, combine 5x Strip of Scaly Skin, 2 Rough Sinew, 1 Simple Sewing Needle [item=68508] and 2 Aligned Steel Thread [item=29191].
You have fashioned the items together to create something new: Stitched Scaly Skin Rug.
Your task 'Skin So Soft [50]' has been updated.
- 4. Deliver the Stitched Scaly Skin Rug 0/1 The Feerrott
You offered 1 Stitched Scaly Skin Rug to Waagosh.
Your task 'Skin So Soft [50]' has been updated.
You complete the trade with Waagosh.
- 5. Talk to Waagosh about the rug 0/1 The Feerrott
You say, 'Hail, Waagosh'
Your task 'Skin So Soft [50]' has been updated.
Waagosh was chuffed to receive her new rug. Now she can treat her feet and move on to more pressing matters.
You have been assigned the task 'No Peeking or Eating'.
Waagosh says, 'Now that is a fine rug indeed. How about you take my [notes] on this here investigation, while I treat my feet?'
You are given: Waagosh's Investigation Notes
This leads into the next task 'No Peeking or Eating [quest=12583]'
Reward(s):
5x Fire-Branded Lizard Kebabs (Food: AC: 15, HP: 275, Mana: 190, End: 300, Rec Level: 90 This is a miraculous meal!)Rewards:

# Tempest Festival: Wander For the Wind

**Level:** 95 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 95
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo

This Tempest Festival task can be obtained from Ulorin Galestrider in Plane of Knowledge on the far west side of the zone near the pool at  /way -18, 1478, -123, next to Roslaind Mischeva. He is on the Find Tool.
You say, 'Hail, Ulorin Galestrider'
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'May the wind guide your [steps] to spread Karana's message far and wide.'
You say, 'Thank you Ulorin. My steps have guided me to you.'
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'Then you've [heard] the same news as I. The rain sings to Rosalind, but to me it warns of a rot that needs to be cleansed from the land. '
You say, 'I have heard that.'
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'I believe that this news has come to me because I like to wander by ship and stream. I was going to head to the Gulf of Gunthak by the Stonebrunt Mountains. Perhaps you [can take] a look while I finish up visiting with my friend?'
You say, 'I can take a look.'
You have been assigned the task 'Wander For the Wind'.
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'May the Tempest guide your steps.
Ulonn Galestnder, apprentice to the Herald of Karana, has heard tales from the rain. The storms speak of rot leaking out into the Gulf of Gunthak. He has asked for your help to investigate these tales and report back to him with your findings.
Investigate Dulak's Harbor for signs of this rotten stain. Check the tunnels filled with hate and gather evidence.
Investigate the Stonebrunt Mountains for signs of the stain of rot. Check the kobold camps near the warrens and gather evidence.
Investigate the Gulf of Gunthak for signs of the stain of corruption. Check the shacks near Dulak's Harbor and gather evidence.
- 1. Find and collect the Stain of Rot in Dulak's Harbor 0/1 Dulak's Harbor
First go through the fake wall at /way 511, -41, 3. Then go to /way 899, -162, -76 in the tunnels. There you will see a stain of rot on the ground as a green pile of ooze. Target it and /open, loot Dulak Stain of Rot.
- 2. Find and collect the Stain of Rot in the Stonebrunt Mountains 0/1 The Stonebrunt Mountains
This one is found at /way -4305, 3395, -42 in a Kobold camp on the far west side of the zone up against the wall. Target it and /open, loot Stonebrunt Stain of Rot
- 3. Find and collect the Stain of Rot in the Gulf of Gunthak 0/1 The Gulf of Gunthak
You can find this one at /way -527, 2504, 69 in a shack not far from the Dulak's Harbor zoneline. Same, target and /open and loot Gunthak Stain of Rot
- 4. Deliver the Stonebrunt Stain of Rot to Ulorin 0/1 Plane of Knowledge
You offered 1 Stonebrunt Stain of Rot to Ulorin Galestrider.
Your task 'Wander For the Wind' has been updated.
- 5. Deliver the Dulak Stain of Rot to Ulorin 0/1 Plane of Knowledge
You offered 1 Dulak Stain of Rot to Ulorin Galestrider.
Your task 'Wander For the Wind' has been updated.
- 6. Deliver the Gunthak Stain of Rot to Ulorin 0/1 Plane of Knowledge
You offered 1 Gunthak Stain of Rot to Ulorin Galestrider.
Your task 'Wander For the Wind' has been updated.
You have wandered the gulf and returned the stain of rot evidence to Ulorin Galestrider.
You complete the trade with Ulorin Galestrider.
You have successfully been granted your reward for: Wander For the Wind
Reward(s):
5 - Rainy Day Ration (AC: 2, HP: 250, Mana: 325, End: 150, 13 to all base stats.
Submitted by: GidonoRewards:
- Rainy Day Ration [item=151430]

# Tempest Festival: Wash Away the Stain

**Level:** 1 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 1
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo

You say, 'Hail, Ulorin Galestrider'
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'Hail, Gebbe. That rot you found, I have never seen anything like it. I can barely keep it [contained].'
You say, 'You can't keep it contained?'
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'Well, no. It chews through everything I put it in. The only thing I have found that works is this relic of Rainkeeper's Stormwater.  It well just destroys [the rot].'
You say, 'I can destroy the rot.'
You have been assigned the task 'Wash Away the Stain'.
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'Oh. Well in that case you will need this. Once you reach the stain release the storm and then let me know what happens.'
You are given: The Rainkeeper's Stormwater
Ulorin Galestrider wants you to return to where you found the rot and try to cleanse it with the Rainkeeper's Stormwater.
Return to Dulak's Harbor and the stain of rot.
Return to the Stonebrunt Mountains and the stain of rot.
Return to Gulf of Gunthak and the stain of rot.
- 1. Return to the site of the stain in Dulak's Harbor 0/1 Dulak's Harbor
Go to the stain at /way 898, -161, -76 which is through the fake wall and the first right, you'll see the green blob on the floor with the plague rats. Stand on top of it to get the location update.
Your task 'Wash Away the Stain' has been updated.
- 2. Release the Rainkeeper's Stormwater 0/1 Dulak's Harbor
Make sure you're standing right on top of it or else it will waste a charge on the potion and it won't take effect on the stain. Target the a stain of rot and right click The Rainkeeper's Stormwater.
I'm releasing the storm onto the stain.
Your task 'Wash Away the Stain' has been updated.
- 3. Return to the site of the stain in the Stonebrunt Mountains 0/1 The Stonebrunt Mountains
This location update is at /way -4305, 3395, -42 on the far west side of the zone up against the wall near a kobold camp. You'll see the stain on the ground.
Your task 'Wash Away the Stain' has been updated.
- 4. Release the Rainkeeper's Stormwater 0/1 The Stonebrunt Mountains
Stand on top of the stain, target it and right click The Rainkeeper's Stormwater.
I'm releasing the storm onto the stain.
Your task 'Wash Away the Stain' has been updated.
- 5. Return to the site of the stain in the Gulf of Gunthak 0/1 The Gulf of Gunthak
This location update is at /way -525, 2501, 72 in the shack north of Dulak's Harbor zoneline.
Your task 'Wash Away the Stain' has been updated.
- 6. Release the Rainkeeper's Stormwater 0/1 The Gulf of Gunthak
Stand on top of the stain, target it and right click The Rainkeeper's Stormwater.
I'm releasing the storm onto the stain.
Your task 'Wash Away the Stain' has been updated.
- 7. Let Ulorin know what you learned 0/1 The Plane of Knowledge
You say, 'Hail, Ulorin Galestrider'
Your task 'Wash Away the Stain' has been updated.
You have used the Rainkeeper's Stormwater on the rot, only for plague rats to gather. Ulorin is not happy with this information and thinks that they have something to do with the spread of he stain.
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'Hello, ______. What happened when you [released] the stormwater?'
You have successfully been granted your reward for: Wash Away the Stain
You have gained 21 ability point(s)!
You have gained 2 mercenary ability point(s)!
You gain experience! (2.000%)

# Tempest Festival: Root Out the Rot

**Level:** 1 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** No | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 1
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** No
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo

This seasonal task starts with Ulorin Galestrider in Plane of Knowledge on the far west side of the zone, he is on the find tool CTRL F.
You say, 'Hail, Ulorin Galestrider'
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'Hello, ______. What happened when you [released] the stormwater?'
You say, 'When I released the stormwater. The stain remained and rats started to gather.'
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'How interesting. This does require some thought. Hrrm. Maybe those pests are contributing to the stain. How would you feel about killing some [of them]?'
You say, 'I can kill some of them.'
You have been assigned the task 'Root Out the Rot'.
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'Maybe getting rid of some of those vermin might get rid of the stain. Please let me know if it helps.'
Ulorin Galestrider wants to cleanse the gulf of the plague rats. Head back to where you found them and exterminate the vile vermin.
- 1. Kill plague rats in Dulak Harbor 0/7 Dulak's Harbor
You can find plague rats in Dulak's Harbor back behind the fake wall that leads to /way 899, -161, -76 to the stain of rot. Plague rats spawn from it and walk off.
You can either kill them as they spawn or find them in the zone and kill them. Once you kill them all, a mouldering rat spawns near the stain of rot.
- 2. Kill the attacking mouldering rat 0/1 Dulak's Harbor
- 3. Kill plague rats in the Stonebrunt Mountains 0/7 The Stonebrunt Mountains
plague rats will spawn at the stain of rot on the far west side of the zone in one of the kobold camps at /way -4305, 3395, -42 and then walk away. You can kill them as they spawn from the stain of rot or find them in the zone.
Once you kill them all, get near the stain of rot and a mouldering rat spawns and attacks, kill it.
- 4. Kill the attacking mouldering rat 0/1 The Stonebrunt Mountains
- 5. Kill plague rats in the Gulf of Gunthak 0/7 The Gulf of Gunthak
The stain of rot is at /way -525, 2501, 72 at a shack north of the Dulak's Harbor zoneline. Plague rats will spawn from it and walk away, kill them there or find them up in the zone.
Once you kill them all, get near the stain of rot and a mouldering rat spawns and attacks, kill it.
- 6. Kill the attacking mouldering rat 0/1 The Gulf of Gunthak
- 7. Let Ulorin know that you killed the rats 0/1 The Plane of Knowledge
You say, 'Hail, Ulorin Galestrider'
Your task 'Root Out the Rot' has been updated.
You have successfully killed the plague and mouldering rats. Ulorin and Rosalind will handle the rest of the clean up from here.
Ulorin Galestrider says, 'More rats, how foul. Rosalind is going to help me cleanse the gulf. Thank you for your assistance. May the wind be always at your back.'
You have successfully been granted your reward for: Root Out the Rot
You have completed achievement: Tempest Festival: From Stagnant to Saltation
Reward(s):
5 - Rainy Day RationSubmitted by: GidonoRewards:
- Rainy Day Ration [item=151430]
